Pseudo:
def newton_flatten(a, target_mode, reg, step):
  N=L size.
  c=conv(a)
  if target_mode flat: r = fill(1.0/L); r[:L] -= c
  else active: r = zeros; active top K where c>threshold; r[active] = T - c[active]
  A = rfft(a,N)
  D = np.zeros_like(A, complex)
  denom = 4*np.abs(A)**2 + reg
  D[1:] = 2*np.conj(A[1:]) * r_fft[1:] / denom
  # Actually need r_fft of r_full length N (real). We can compute R = rfft(r_full,N)
  R = rfft(r_full,N)
  D[1:] = 2*np.conj(A[1:]) * R[1:] / denom
  D[0]=0
  delta = irfft(D,N)[:n]
  # step maybe line search
  for step in [1,0.5,...]:
    a_new = a + step*delta
    if a_new.min()<0: maybe scale? We can clip but better reduce step until min>=0.
    a_new = max(a_new,0); if sum>0 normalize.
    score = conv_new max*2n? (normalized, M*2n)
    if score<b: accept.
  return best.

def flatten_one(a, N, target_flat, kappa=1.0, reg_scale=1e-8, max_step=1.0):
  n=len(a), L=2*n-1
  c = conv_scores maybe
  M0 = c.max()
  # target residual
  r_full = np.zeros(N)
  if target_flat:
    flat = 1.0 / L
    r_full[:L] = kappa*(flat - c)
  else active:
    # T = flat? We can choose T = max(flat, M0 - delta?) maybe.
  R = np.fft.rfft(r_full, N)
  A = np.fft.rfft(a, N)
  D = np.zeros_like(A, complex)
  amag2 = np.abs(A)**2
  # zero DC? Need R[0] maybe small; set 0.
  R[0]=0.0
  denom = 4*amag2 + reg
  # reg choose: reg = reg_scale * max(amag2[1:])
  if reg is None: reg=reg_scale*np.max(amag2[1:]) if len>1 else 1
  D[1:] = (2*np.conj(A[1:]) * R[1:]) / denom
  D[0]=0
  delta = np.fft.irfft(D, N)[:n]
  # optional: subtract mean small
  delta -= delta.mean()
  # line search over eta (step)
  best_a=a; best_score=2*n*M0
  for eta in [1.0,0.7,0.5,0.35,0.25,0.15,0.1,0.05,0.02]:
    cand = a + eta*delta
    if cand.min() < 0: 
       # Instead of skip, we can clip if eta small enough? Skip if min > -tiny? 
       # If min < -1e-12 skip; else clip
    cand = np.maximum(cand,0)
    s = cand.sum()
    if s<=0: continue
    cand /= s
    c2=conv; M2=c2.max(); sc=2n M2
    if sc<best: accept.
  return best, best_score

Let's write build_delta(a, c, N, flat_strength, reg_scale):
  n=len(a); L=2*n-1
  flat = 1.0/L
  r = np.zeros(N)
  r[:L] = flat_strength * (flat - c)
  # Ensure sum zero (numeric): subtract mean? Since D0=0, residual DC ignored.
  R = np.fft.rfft(r, N)
  R[0]=0
  A = np.fft.rfft(a, N)
  amag2 = np.abs(A)**2
  # floor
  # If amag2 has zeros, set floor
  if reg_scale is None: reg = 1e-10 * max(1.0, np.max(amag2))
  else: reg = reg_scale * np.max(amag2)
  # But A0=1, max=1; reg relative to A0 too large for lowfreq (1e-10) okay? amag2 lowfreq maybe 1/n, so reg 1e-10 smaller; good. For A small highfreq zero, reg 1e-10 may dominate.
  denom = 4*amag2 + reg
  D=np.zeros_like(A,complex)
  # Avoid division by zero at DC
  D[1:] = 2*np.conj(A[1:]) * R[1:] / denom[1:]
  delta = np.fft.irfft(D,N)[:n]
  delta -= delta.mean()
  # Scale delta to reasonable step? The normal equation solution for residual to target may be large because target difference ~1/n; delta could be O(a) maybe. We can scale: if np.max(np.abs(delta)) > 2*np.max(a): delta *= 2*np.max(a)/maxabs. But line search handles. Maybe scale to RMS: delta_rms / a_rms. Use delta *= min(1, 0.5 / (maxabs/(maxa+1e-15))) to avoid huge negative. Let's cap relative L2: if ||delta||2 > 0.5||a||2: delta *=0.5||a||/||delta||. Since a sum1, ||a||~1/√n? Good.
  delta -= delta.mean()
  return delta

def lowdim_objective(z):
    x=np.asarray(z)
    # softmax stable
    xu=x-x.max()
    ax=np.exp(xu)
    s=ax.sum(); a=ax/s
    c=np.convolve(a,a)
    return 2*len(a)*c.max()

def mgd_top(a0, time_limit, top_k=40, eta0=0.03, verbose=False):
  a=norm_sum1(a0); n=len(a); N=pow2; best...
  eta=eta0; patience=0; stall=0
  while time<limit:
    c=conv(a); M=c.max(); score=2nM
    update best
    top_idx=np.argsort(c)[::-1][:top_k]
    weights = c[top_idx]**2 / sum
    grad=zeros
    for w,k: start... grad[idx] += 2*w*a[k-idx]
    grad-=grad.mean()
    gmax=...; grad/=gmax
    for eta_try in [eta, eta*0.5, eta*0.25, eta*0.1]:
       a_new = a*np.exp(-eta_try*grad); normalize
       c_new=conv; if score_new < best_score - tol accept and break
    if accept: eta*=0.998, patience=0
    else: eta*=0.5; patience++; if patience>200 break or random restart
  return best

def newton_optimize(a0, time_limit, verbose=False):
  a=norm...
  best...
  N...
  attempt_idx=0; stall=0; max_stall=60
  attempt_patterns = [ ... ]
  while time>t:
    c=conv(a); score...
    if improved update
    if score < best_score* (1-1e-7): maybe keep
    if score < TARGET? no.
    # choose pattern
    pat = patterns[attempt_idx % len(patterns)]
    mode, rho, strength, reg_scale = pat
    r = make_residual(c, mode, rho, strength)
    delta = solve_delta(a,r,N,reg_scale)
    # scale delta
    scale = 0.5*norm(a)/norm(delta) if norm(delta)>0 else 1; delta *= min(max(scale,0.05),2.0)
    accepted=False
    for step in [1.0,0.5,0.25,0.1,0.05,0.02]:
      cand = a + step*delta
      cand = np.maximum(cand,0)
      s=cand.sum(); if s<... continue
      cand/=s
      c2=conv(cand); sc=2n*c2.max()
      if sc < best_score - 1e-12:
         a=cand; best...; accepted=True; break
    if accepted:
       attempt_idx=0; stall=0
    else:
       attempt_idx += 1; stall +=1
       if stall > max_stall:
          # random perturbation
          noise=0.02*np.random.default_rng(seed).normal(size=n)
          a = a*np.exp(0.01*noise); a=norm; stall=0; max_stall=...
    it++
  return best

Now, internal conv function:

def conv_normalized(a, N=None):
  n=len(a); L=2*n-1
  if N is None: N=next_pow2(L)
  A=np.fft.rfft(a,N)
  c=np.fft.irfft(A*A,N)[:L]
  c=np.maximum(c,0.0)
  s=c.sum()
  if s>0: c=c/s
  else:
    c=np.zeros(L); c[L//2]=1
  return c

This renormalizes to sum1, so if a sum1 but numerical, okay. The score M = c.max. Since c scaled by 1/s, if s slightly !=1, objective scale? If a sum not exactly1 due norm, c sum should s_a^2. Renormalizing c to1 corresponds to using normalized a. Good.

`score_sequence(a)` could use this. But if a has sum not1 due candidate_score, we normalize a first.

Now coordinate/block local. Let's include a simple `stochastic_block(a0, time_limit)`:

- For iterations while time:
   c=conv; M; score update.
   top_lags=argsort(c,)[::-1][:12]
   improved=False
   for attempt in range(40):
      # choose a random block near a top lag's contributing region
      k=top_lags[attempt%len]
      s=max(0,k-n+1); e=min(n-1,k); center=(s+e)//2
      if attempt<20: start=center+int(rng.integers(-block_size,block_size))
      else: start=rng.integers(0,n-block_size)
      start=clip
      block=a[start:end]
      # perturb multiplicatively to preserve positivity: new_block = block * exp(noise)
      noise=rng.normal(0,0.1,end-start)
      new_block=block*np.exp(noise)
      a_temp=a.copy(); a_temp[start:end]=new_block; normalize; score.
      accept if better.
   if not improved:
      # random mass transfer small
      i=rng.integers(n); j=rng.integers(n); amt=0.01*a[i]; a[i]-=amt; a[j]+=amt; normalize; score; if not better revert.
- return best.

```
def update_best(best_seq, best_score, cand):
```

Let's write alpha function efficiently:
```
def make_alpha(n):
    a=np.empty(n)
    val=1.0
    for i in range(n):
       a[i]=val
       val *= (2*i+1)/(2*(i+1))
    return a
```

Let's revisit continuous optimization via "cap" Newton. It essentially tries to solve c = target. If target cap lowers active lags, it may converge to a local minimum. - T = M*(1-rho)
- u = max(c-T,0)
- low_mask = c < T - 2*rho*M? or c < 0.9*T.
- comp = u.sum() / low_mask.sum()
- r=-u on high; r += comp on low_mask. Sum zero. This reduces high, raises only low lags, leaving moderate lags unchanged. This may be safer.

Residual function:
```
def make_residual(c, mode, rho, strength):
  L=c.size; M=c.max(); flat=1/L
  r=zeros
  if mode==0: #flat
     r=strength*(flat-c)
  else:
     T = M*(1-rho) if rho is not None else M - abs_delta
     high = c > T
     u = np.maximum(c-T,0)
     total = u.sum()
     if total>0:
        r[high] = -u[high]
        # choose low mask c < T*(1-2*rho) maybe
        low_thr = max(0, T*(1-2*rho)) if rho<0.5 else T*0.5
        low = c < low_thr
        if low.sum()==0: low = ~high
        r[low] += total / low.sum()
     r *= strength
  # Center DC by setting r -= r.mean()? But operator ignores DC if D0=0. target sum 1? r sum maybe 0; numeric subtract mean to be safe.
  r -= r.mean()
  return r
```
For alpha, low_mask excludes near T; raises very low tail. This can reduce high without creating new max nearby. Good. Use capdist as mode 1.

Let's write `make_residual(c, mode, rho, strength)`:
```
def make_residual(c, mode, rho, strength):
    L=len(c); M=float(c.max()); flat=1.0/L
    r=np.zeros(L)
    if mode == 0:
        r[:]=strength*(flat - c)
    else:
        if rho is None: rho=0.02
        T = M * (1.0 - rho)
        if T < 0: T = 0.0
        high = c > T
        u = np.maximum(c - T, 0.0)
        total = float(u.sum())
        if total > 1e-300:
            r[high] = -u[high]
            if rho < 0.4:
                thr = T * (1.0 - max(0.1, 1.5*rho))
            else:
                thr = T * 0.3
            low = c < thr
            if not np.any(low):
                low = ~high
            if np.any(low):
                r[low] += total / float(np.count_nonzero(low))
    r[r<0 and? no]
    r -= r.mean()
    return r
```
For mode=1, if high all (c>T) and low none? Not.

Newton delta:
```
def newton_delta(a, r, N, reg_scale):
    n=len(a)
    R=np.fft.rfft(r, N)  # r length 2n-1, N >= 2n-1, zero pad ok
    R[0]=0.0
    A=np.fft.rfft(a, N)
    amag2=np.abs(A)**2
    if len(amag2)>1:
        base=max(1e-16, float(np.max(amag2[1:])))
    else: base=1.0
    if reg_scale is None or reg_scale <=0:
        reg=1e-12*base
    else:
        reg=reg_scale*base
    reg=max(reg,1e-15)
    D=np.zeros_like(A, dtype=complex)
    denom=4.0*amag2 + reg
    D[1:] = (2.0*np.conj(A[1:]) * R[1:]) / denom[1:]
    delta=np.fft.irfft(D, N)[:n]
    delta -= np.mean(delta)
    return delta
```
Note r length L, N may be >L; np.fft.rfft(r,N) zero pads. Good.

Newton optimize:

```
def newton_optimize(a0, time_limit, verbose=False):
   a=norm_sum1(a0)
   n=len(a); N=next_pow2(2*n-1)
   c=conv_normalized(a,N); best_score=2*n*c.max(); best_a=a.copy()
   t0=time.monotonic()
   patterns = [...] maybe global list
   idx=0; stall=0; it=0
   rng=np.random.default_rng(987)
   while time.monotonic()-t0 < time_limit:
       c=conv_normalized(a,N); M=c.max(); score=2*n*M
       if score < best_score - 1e-14: update
       # if time check
       pat = PATTERNS_NEWTON[idx % len]
       r=make_residual(c, pat[0], pat[1], pat[2])
       delta = newton_delta(a,r,N,pat[3])
       # scale
       dn=np.linalg.norm(delta); an=np.linalg.norm(a)
       if dn>1e-18:
           factor=0.5*an/dn
           if factor>2: factor=2
           elif factor<0.02: factor=0.02
           delta*=factor
       accepted=False
       for step in (1.0,0.5,0.25,0.1,0.05,0.02):
           cand=a+step*delta
           # if too many negative? skip? 
           if cand.min() < -1e-8*max(1.0, np.max(a)):
                # still try clip? maybe skip for larger step
                if step >=0.5: continue
           cand=np.maximum(cand,0.0)
           s=cand.sum()
           if s<1e-15: continue
           cand/=s
           c2=conv_normalized(cand,N); sc=2*n*c2.max()
           if sc < best_score - 1e-13:
               a=cand; best_score=sc; best_a=cand.copy(); accepted=True; break
       if accepted:
           idx=0; stall=0
       else:
           idx +=1; stall +=1
           if stall == 30:
               # random multiplicative perturbation
               noise=rng.normal(0.0, 0.05, n)
               a = a*np.exp(noise)
               a=norm_sum1(a)
               # re-evaluate best? maybe not
           if stall > 80:
               break
       it +=1
       if verbose and it % 200 ==0:
           print(...)
   return best_a
```

Now MGD function. Let's adapt previous but optimize for top-lag. We should use normalized a and conv with N. The gradient direct:
```
def mgd_top(a0, time_limit, top_k=40, eta0=0.03):
 a=norm; n; N; best; it=0; patience=0; eta=eta0
 rng...
 while time<limit:
   c=conv(a,N); score=2n cmax; update
   top_idx=np.argsort(c)[::-1][:top_k]
   top_vals=c[top_idx]
   w=top_vals**2; w/=w.sum()
   grad=np.zeros(n)
   for wi,k in zip(w, top_idx):
      k=int(k); s=max(0,k-n+1); e=min(n,k+1)
      idx=np.arange(s,e)
      grad[idx] += 2.0*wi*a[k-idx]
   grad-=grad.mean(); gmax=np.abs(grad).max(); if gmax<1e-18 break; grad/=gmax
   accepted=False
   for step in (eta, eta*0.5, eta*0.25, eta*0.1, 0.005):
      cand=a*np.exp(-step*grad)
      cand=np.maximum(cand,0); normalize; c2; sc
      if sc<best_score: accept
   if accepted: eta*=0.999; patience=0
   else: eta*=0.5; patience+=1; if patience>100: random mass; if patience>300 break
```
Multiplicative exp(-eta*grad). grad normalized to max1. eta maybe 0.05. For nonconvex, okay.

Now, lowdim_search. Need avoid calling if no scipy? We can guard:
```
def lowdim_search(ref_a, time_limit):
  if spopt is None: return None
  m=48
  best=None; best_score=inf
  starts=[]
  if ref_a is not None:
      a_down=np.interp(np.linspace(0,1,m), np.linspace(0,1,len(ref_a)), ref_a); starts.append(norm_sum1(a_down))
  starts.append(make_alpha(m))
  x=np.linspace(0,1,m); starts.append(norm_sum1(np.sin(np.pi*x)**2+0.2))
  starts.append(norm_sum1(np.exp(-((x-0.25)/0.2)**2)+0.1))
  t0=time.monotonic(); rng=np.random.default_rng(5)
  for idx,a0 in enumerate(starts):
     if time.monotonic()-t0 > time_limit: break
     z=np.log(a0+1e-30)
     z = z - z[0]
     z += 0.05*rng.standard_normal(m)
     try:
       res=spopt.minimize(lowdim_objective, z, method='Nelder-Mead', options={'maxiter':500, 'xatol':1e-5, 'fatol':1e-7, 'adaptive':True})
       ar=softmax_proba(res.x); sc=...; update
     except Exception: pass
  return best
```

Softmax:
```
def softmax_proba(z):
    z=np.asarray(z, dtype=float)
    if z.size == 0: return np.empty(0)
    z = z - np.max(z)
    e=np.exp(z)
    s=e.sum()
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0:
        return np.ones(z.size)/z.size
    return e/s
```

`norm_sum1`:
```
def norm_sum1(a):
    a=np.asarray(a,dtype=float)
    a=np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a=np.maximum(a,0.0)
    s=a.sum()
    if s<=1e-15:
       if a.size: return np.ones(a.size)/a.size
       return np.empty(0)
    return a/s
```

`conv_normalized`:
```
def conv_normalized(a, N=None):
    a=np.asarray(a,float)
    n=a.size
    if n==0: return None
    L=2*n-1
    if N is None: N=next_pow2(L)
    A=np.fft.rfft(a,N)
    c=np.fft.irfft(A*A,N)[:L]
    c=np.maximum(c,0.0)
    s=c.sum()
    if s <= 0:
        c=np.zeros(L); c[L//2]=1.0
    else:
        c = c / s
    return c
```
Rfft of real a, A*A correct for linear conv because nonnegative real; no conjugate needed. For real, FFT squared is circular convolution of a with a (not autocorrelation) because both nonnegative and same sequence. Good. If a has zeros, fine.

`score_normalized`:
```
def score_normalized(a):
    a=norm_sum1(a)
    n=a.size
    if n==0: return None, inf
    c=conv_normalized(a)
    M=float(c.max())
    return a, float(2.0*n*M)
```

`update_best`:
```
def update_best(best_seq, best_score, cand):
    if cand is None: return best_seq,best_score
    q,sc=score_normalized(cand)
    if q is not None and np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
        return q,sc
    return best_seq,best_score
```
This recomputes; if cand already best, okay.

`make_alpha`:
```
def make_alpha(n):
    a=np.empty(n)
    if n>0:
      a[0]=1.0
      val=1.0
      for i in range(1,n):
         val *= (2*i - 1)/(2*i)
         a[i]=val
    return a
```
Wait recurrence: α_i = α_{i-1} * (2i-1)/(2i). For i=1: α1=1*1/2=0.5. For i=2:0.5*3/4=0.375. Good.

`make_alpha_weighted`:
```
def make_alpha_weighted(n, b1=0.0, b2=0.0, b3=0.0):
    a=make_alpha(n)
    if n>1:
      x=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,n)
    else: x=np.array([0.0])
    w=1.0 + b1*x + b2*x*(1.0-x) + b3*x*x
    a = a * np.maximum(w, 1e-8)
    return a
```

`interpolate_sequence` as previous with positivity and norm.

Lowdim:
```
def lowdim_objective(z):
    z=np.asarray(z,float)
    if z.size ==0: return 1e100
    z=z-z[0]
    e=np.exp(z)
    s=e.sum()
    if s<=0 or not np.isfinite(s): return 1e100
    a=e/s
    c=np.convolve(a,a)
    c=np.maximum(c,0.0)
    cs=c.sum()
    if cs>0: c=c/cs
    return float(2.0*a.size*c.max())
```
No scipy callback. Nelder may pass list; fine.

`lowdim_search`:
```
def lowdim_search(ref_a, time_limit):
    if spopt is None: return None
    m=48
    best=None; best_sc=float('inf')
    starts=[]
    if ref_a is not None and len(ref_a)>1:
       old_n=len(ref_a)
       x_old=np.linspace(0,1,old_n); x_new=np.linspace(0,1,m)
       down=np.interp(x_new,x_old,ref_a)
       starts.append(norm_sum1(down))
    starts.append(make_alpha(m))
    x=np.linspace(0,1,m)
    starts.append(norm_sum1(np.sin(np.pi*x)**2 + 0.1))
    starts.append(norm_sum1(np.exp(-((x-0.3)**2)/0.1)+0.1))
    t0=time.monotonic(); rng=np.random.default_rng(17)
    for idx,a0 in enumerate(starts):
       if time.monotonic()-t0 > time_limit: break
       a0=norm_sum1(a0)
       # ensure positive
       a0=np.maximum(a0,1e-300)
       z=np.log(a0); z=z-z[0]; z += 0.05*rng.standard_normal(m)
       try:
          res=spopt.minimize(lowdim_objective, z, method='Nelder-Mead', options={'maxiter':300, 'xatol':1e-5, 'fatol':1e-8, 'adaptive':True})
          ar=softmax_proba(res.x)
          c=np.convolve(ar,ar); c=np.maximum(c,0); c/=c.sum(); sc=2*m*c.max()
          if sc < best_sc: best=ar; best_sc=sc
          # also original maybe
       except Exception:
          pass
    return best
```
Potential: Nelder-Mead with 48 dims and maxiter300 may be slow but okay. If time_limit smaller, starts less.

Let's write `block_search` code:
```
def block_search(a0, time_limit, block_size=None):
    a=norm_sum1(a0)
    n=a.size
    if n==0: return a
    N=next_pow2(2*n-1)
    best_a=a.copy(); best_score=...; t0=...
    if block_size is None:
        block_size=max(8, min(128, n//20))
    block_size=min(block_size, max(2, n//4))
    rng=np.random.default_rng(7)
    stall=0
    while time.monotonic()-t0 < time_limit and stall < 150:
       c=conv_normalized(a,N); cur=... update
       if cur<1e-? no
       top_lags=np.argsort(c)[::-1][:12]
       improved=False
       for attempt in range(60):
          if time... break
          if attempt<24 and top_lags.size:
             k=int(top_lags[attempt % top_lags.size])
             s=max(0,k-n+1); e=min(n-1,k); center=(s+e)//2
             offset=int(rng.integers(-block_size, block_size+1))
             start=center+offset
          else:
             start=int(rng.integers(0, max(1, n-block_size+1)))
          start=max(0,min(start,n-block_size))
          end=start+block_size
          block=a[start:end].copy()
          if block.sum() <= 1e-18: continue
          noise=rng.normal(0.0,0.12,end-start)
          new_block=block*np.exp(noise)
          cand=a.copy(); cand[start:end]=new_block; cand=np.maximum(cand,0); ssum=cand.sum(); if ssum<=... continue; cand/=ssum
          c2=conv_normalized(cand,N); sc=2*n*c2.max()
          if sc < min(best_score, cur) - 1e-14:
              a=cand; cur=sc; improved=True
              if sc<best_score: best_score=sc; best_a=cand.copy()
       if not improved:
          i=int(rng.integers(n)); j=int(rng.integers(n))
          if i!=j and a[i]>1e-15:
             amt=0.01*a[i]
             cand=a.copy(); cand[i]-=amt; cand[j]+=amt; cand=norm; sc=...
             if sc < min(best_score, cur): a=cand; cur=sc; improved? (update best)
          stall +=1
          if stall == 50: a=best_a.copy()
          if stall > 120: break
    return best_a
```
This may be slow due 60 conv per iter; but time-limited. Use only if remaining. MGD maybe better. We can reduce attempts 30. Okay.

```python
"""
Search for non-negative sequences minimizing
    2*n*max(conv(a,a)) / sum(a)^2.

The method combines:
  * strong analytic/random initializers (central-binomial "alpha" family,
    weighted alpha variants, Low-dimensional Nelder-Mead refinement),
  * a Gauss-Newton style flattening step on the linear convolution,
  * active-lag multiplicative subgradient descent,
  * lightweight stochastic block/mass-transfer polishing.
"""

import numpy as np
import time

try:
    from scipy import optimize as spopt
except Exception:
    spopt = None


NEWTON_PATTERNS = [
    # (mode, rho, strength, reg_scale)
    # mode 1: capped-current target with compensation on low lags
    # mode 0: flat target 1/(2n-1)
    (1, 0.02, 1.00, 1e-8),
    (1, 0.05, 1.00, 0.0),
    (1, 0.01, 1.00, 1e-10),
    (0, 0.00, 1.00, 0.0),
    (1, 0.08, 0.80, 1e-6),
    (0, 0.00, 0.50, 1e-8),
    (1, 0.03, 1.00, 1e-6),
    (1, 0.10, 1.00, 0.0),
    (0, 0.00, 0.70, 0.0),
]


def next_pow2(m):
    n = 1
    while n < m:
        n <<= 1
    return n


def norm_sum1(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    if a.ndim != 1:
        a = a.reshape(-1)
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s <= 1e-15:
        if a.size:
            return np.ones(a.size, dtype=np.float64) / a.size
        return np.empty(0, dtype=np.float64)
    return a / s


def softmax_proba(z):
    z = np.asarray(z, dtype=np.float64)
    if z.size == 0:
        return np.empty(0, dtype=np.float64)
    z = z - float(np.max(z))
    e = np.exp(z)
    s = float(e.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        return np.ones(z.size, dtype=np.float64) / z.size
    return e / s


def conv_normalized(a, N=None):
    """FFT linear convolution of a with itself, normalized to sum 1."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return None
    L = 2 * n - 1
    if N is None:
        N = next_pow2(L)
    A = np.fft.rfft(a, N)
    c = np.fft.irfft(A * A, N)[:L]
    c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)
    s = float(c.sum())
    if s <= 0.0:
        c = np.zeros(L, dtype=np.float64)
        c[L // 2] = 1.0
    else:
        c = c / s
    return c


def score_normalized(a):
    """Return (normalized_sequence, internal_score) or (None, inf)."""
    a = norm_sum1(a)
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return None, float("inf")
    c = conv_normalized(a)
    if c is None:
        return None, float("inf")
    M = float(c.max())
    if not np.isfinite(M):
        return None, float("inf")
    return a, float(2.0 * n * M)


def update_best(best_seq, best_score, cand):
    if cand is None:
        return best_seq, best_score
    q, sc = score_normalized(cand)
    if q is not None and np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
        return q, sc
    return best_seq, best_score


def make_alpha(n):
    """Central binomial coefficients alpha_i = binom(2i,i)/4^i."""
    a = np.empty(n, dtype=np.float64)
    if n == 0:
        return a
    a[0] = 1.0
    val = 1.0
    for i in range(1, n):
        val *= (2.0 * i - 1.0) / (2.0 * i)
        a[i] = val
    return a


def make_alpha_weighted(n, b1=0.0, b2=0.0, b3=0.0):
    a = make_alpha(n)
    if n <= 1:
        return a
    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    w = 1.0 + b1 * x + b2 * x * (1.0 - x) + b3 * x * x
    a = a * np.maximum(w, 1e-8)
    return a


def interpolate_sequence(f, new_n):
    f = norm_sum1(f)
    old_n = f.size
    if old_n == 0:
        return np.ones(int(new_n), dtype=np.float64) / int(new_n)
    new_n = int(new_n)
    if old_n == new_n:
        return f.copy()
    x_old = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, old_n)
    x_new = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, new_n)
    y = np.interp(x_new, x_old, f)
    y = np.maximum(y, 0.0)
    s = float(y.sum())
    if s <= 1e-15:
        return np.ones(new_n, dtype=np.float64) / new_n
    return y / s


def lowdim_objective(z):
    z = np.asarray(z, dtype=np.float64)
    if z.size == 0:
        return 1e100
    z = z - z[0]
    e = np.exp(z)
    s = float(e.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        return 1e100
    a = e / s
    c = np.convolve(a, a)
    c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)
    cs = float(c.sum())
    if cs <= 0.0:
        return 1e100
    c = c / cs
    return float(2.0 * a.size * c.max())


def lowdim_search(ref_a, time_limit):
    """Small-dimensional Nelder-Mead shape search, then return best low-dim vector."""
    if spopt is None or time_limit <= 0.0:
        return None

    m = 48
    best = None
    best_sc = float("inf")
    starts = []

    if ref_a is not None and ref_a.size >= 2:
        x_old = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, ref_a.size)
        x_new = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, m)
        down = np.interp(x_new, x_old, ref_a)
        starts.append(norm_sum1(down))

    starts.append(make_alpha(m))

    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, m)
    starts.append(norm_sum1(np.sin(np.pi * x) ** 2 + 0.1))
    starts.append(norm_sum1(np.exp(-((x - 0.3) ** 2) / 0.10) + 0.1))
    starts.append(norm_sum1(np.exp(-((x - 0.7) ** 2) / 0.10) + 0.1))

    t0 = time.monotonic()
    rng = np.random.default_rng(2718)

    for a0 in starts:
        if time.monotonic() - t0 > time_limit:
            break
        a0 = np.maximum(norm_sum1(a0), 1e-300)
        z = np.log(a0)
        z = z - z[0]
        z = z + 0.05 * rng.standard_normal(m)

        try:
            res = spopt.minimize(
                lowdim_objective,
                z,
                method="Nelder-Mead",
                options={"maxiter": 250, "xatol": 1e-5, "fatol": 1e-8, "adaptive": True},
            )
            ar = softmax_proba(res.x)
            c = np.convolve(ar, ar)
            c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)
            cs = float(c.sum())
            if cs > 0.0:
                c = c / cs
                sc = float(2.0 * m * c.max())
                if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc:
                    best = ar.copy()
                    best_sc = sc
        except Exception:
            pass

    return best


def make_residual(c, mode, rho, strength):
    """Build a zero-mean residual vector for the Gauss-Newton flattening step."""
    c = np.asarray(c, dtype=np.float64)
    L = c.size
    M = float(c.max())
    r = np.zeros(L, dtype=np.float64)
    if not np.isfinite(M) or M <= 0.0:
        return r

    if mode == 0:
        flat = 1.0 / L
        r[:] = strength * (flat - c)
    else:
        # Cap lags above T, compensate only sufficiently low lags.
        if rho is None:
            rho = 0.02
        rho = float(rho)
        T = M * (1.0 - rho)
        if T < 0.0:
            T = 0.0

        high = c > T
        u = np.maximum(c - T, 0.0)
        total = float(u.sum())
        if total > 1e-300:
            r[high] = -u[high]

            if rho < 0.35:
                thr = T * (1.0 - max(0.20, 2.0 * rho))
            else:
                thr = 0.30 * T
            thr = max(0.0, thr)

            low = c < thr
            if not np.any(low):
                low = c < T
            if not np.any(low):
                low = c < (M + 1.0) / 2.0

            cnt = int(np.count_nonzero(low))
            if cnt > 0:
                r[low] += total / float(cnt)

        r *= strength

    r -= float(r.mean())
    return r


def newton_delta(a, r, N, reg_scale):
    """
    Solve the regularized frequency-domain normal equations for
        min_delta || 2 * conv(a, delta) - r ||^2 + reg ||delta||^2,
    with zero-mean delta.
    """
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    r = np.asarray(r, dtype=np.float64)
    n = a.size

    R = np.fft.rfft(r, N)
    R[0] = 0.0
    A = np.fft.rfft(a, N)
    amag2 = np.abs(A) ** 2

    if amag2.size > 1:
        base = float(np.max(amag2[1:]))
    else:
        base = 1.0
    base = max(1e-18, base)

    if reg_scale is None or reg_scale <= 0.0:
        reg = 1e-12 * base
    else:
        reg = float(reg_scale) * base
    reg = max(reg, 1e-15)

    D = np.zeros_like(A, dtype=np.complex128)
    denom = 4.0 * amag2 + reg
    if A.size > 1:
        D[1:] = (2.0 * np.conj(A[1:]) * R[1:]) / denom[1:]

    delta = np.fft.irfft(D, N)[:n]
    delta -= float(delta.mean())
    return delta


def newton_optimize(a0, time_limit, verbose=False):
    a = norm_sum1(a0)
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return a
    N = next_pow2(2 * n - 1)

    c0 = conv_normalized(a, N)
    best_score = float(2.0 * n * c0.max())
    best_a = a.copy()
    working = a.copy()

    t0 = time.monotonic()
    idx = 0
    stall = 0
    iteration = 0
    rng = np.random.default_rng(31415)

    while time.monotonic() - t0 < time_limit:
        c = conv_normalized(working, N)
        cur_score = float(2.0 * n * c.max())
        if cur_score < best_score and np.isfinite(cur_score):
            best_score = cur_score
            best_a = working.copy()

        pat = NEWTON_PATTERNS[idx % len(NEWTON_PATTERNS)]
        mode, rho, strength, reg_scale = pat
        r = make_residual(c, mode, rho, strength)
        delta = newton_delta(working, r, N, reg_scale)

        dn = float(np.linalg.norm(delta))
        an = float(np.linalg.norm(working))
        if dn > 1e-18 and an > 1e-18:
            factor = 0.5 * an / dn
            factor = min(2.0, max(0.02, factor))
            delta = delta * factor

        accepted = False
        # Line search. Accept improvements over working, update best if stronger.
        for step in (1.0, 0.5, 0.25, 0.10, 0.05, 0.02):
            cand = working + step * delta

            # Very large negative components are usually amplified by projection;
            # still try smallest steps by clipping.
            cand = np.maximum(cand, 0.0)
            s = float(cand.sum())
            if s <= 1e-15:
                continue
            cand = cand / s

            c2 = conv_normalized(cand, N)
            sc = float(2.0 * n * c2.max())
            threshold = min(best_score, cur_score)
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < threshold - 1e-14:
                working = cand
                cur_score = sc
                accepted = True
                if sc < best_score:
                    best_score = sc
                    best_a = cand.copy()
                break

        if accepted:
            stall = 0
            idx = 0
        else:
            idx = (idx + 1) % len(NEWTON_PATTERNS)
            stall += 1
            if stall == 20:
                working = best_a.copy()
            elif stall == 50:
                noise = rng.normal(0.0, 0.04, n)
                working = best_a * np.exp(noise)
                working = norm_sum1(working)
                stall = 0
                idx = int(rng.integers(len(NEWTON_PATTERNS)))
            elif stall > 140:
                break

        iteration += 1
        if verbose and iteration % 250 == 0:
            print(
                "    Newton iter=%d elapsed=%.0fs best=%.6f"
                % (iteration, time.monotonic() - t0, best_score)
            )

    return best_a


def mgd_top(a0, time_limit, top_k=45, eta0=0.035):
    """Active-lag multiplicative gradient descent on the simplex."""
    a = norm_sum1(a0)
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return a

    N = next_pow2(2 * n - 1)
    c0 = conv_normalized(a, N)
    best_score = float(2.0 * n * c0.max())
    best_a = a.copy()
    working = a.copy()

    t0 = time.monotonic()
    eta = float(eta0)
    stall = 0
    iteration = 0
    rng = np.random.default_rng(555)
    top_k = int(min(max(8, top_k), max(16, n // 50)))

    while time.monotonic() - t0 < time_limit:
        c = conv_normalized(working, N)
        cur_score = float(2.0 * n * c.max())
        if cur_score < best_score and np.isfinite(cur_score):
            best_score = cur_score
            best_a = working.copy()

        top_idx = np.argsort(c)[::-1][:top_k]
        top_vals = np.maximum(c[top_idx], 1e-300)
        w = top_vals * top_vals
        ws = float(w.sum())
        if ws <= 1e-300:
            w = np.ones_like(w) / max(1, w.size)
        else:
            w = w / ws

        grad = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
        for wi, k in zip(w, top_idx):
            ki = int(k)
            s = max(0, ki - n + 1)
            e = min(n, ki + 1)
            if s >= e:
                continue
            idx = np.arange(s, e)
            grad[idx] += 2.0 * wi * working[ki - idx]

        grad -= float(grad.mean())
        gmax = float(np.abs(grad).max())
        if gmax <= 1e-18:
            break
        grad /= gmax

        accepted = False
        for step in (eta, eta * 0.5, eta * 0.25, eta * 0.10, 0.004):
            cand = working * np.exp(-step * grad)
            cand = np.maximum(cand, 0.0)
            s = float(cand.sum())
            if s <= 1e-15:
                continue
            cand = cand / s

            c2 = conv_normalized(cand, N)
            sc = float(2.0 * n * c2.max())
            threshold = min(best_score, cur_score)
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < threshold - 1e-14:
                working = cand
                cur_score = sc
                accepted = True
                if sc < best_score:
                    best_score = sc
                    best_a = cand.copy()
                break

        if accepted:
            stall = 0
            eta = min(0.12, eta * 0.999)
        else:
            eta *= 0.5
            if eta < 0.002:
                eta = 0.025
                top_k = max(10, top_k // 2)
            stall += 1
            if stall == 30:
                working = best_a.copy()
                stall = 0
            elif stall > 160:
                break

        iteration += 1
        if time.monotonic() - t0 > time_limit:
            break

    return best_a


def block_search(a0, time_limit, block_size=None):
    """Stochastic multiplicative block perturbations."""
    a = norm_sum1(a0)
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return a

    N = next_pow2(2 * n - 1)
    c0 = conv_normalized(a, N)
    best_score = float(2.0 * n * c0.max())
    best_a = a.copy()
    working = a.copy()

    t0 = time.monotonic()
    if block_size is None:
        block_size = max(8, min(128, n // 20))
    block_size = int(min(block_size, max(2, n // 4)))

    rng = np.random.default_rng(99)
    stall = 0

    while time.monotonic() - t0 < time_limit and stall < 160:
        c = conv_normalized(working, N)
        cur_score = float(2.0 * n * c.max())
        if cur_score < best_score and np.isfinite(cur_score):
            best_score = cur_score
            best_a = working.copy()

        top_lags = np.argsort(c)[::-1][:12]
        improved = False

        for attempt in range(36):
            if time.monotonic() - t0 > time_limit:
                break

            if attempt < 18 and top_lags.size:
                ki = int(top_lags[attempt % top_lags.size])
                s = max(0, ki - n + 1)
                e = min(n - 1, ki)
                center = (s + e) // 2
                offset = int(rng.integers(-block_size, block_size + 1))
                start = center + offset
            else:
                start = int(rng.integers(0, max(1, n - block_size + 1)))

            start = max(0, min(start, n - block_size))
            end = start + block_size
            block = working[start:end].copy()
            if float(block.sum()) <= 1e-18:
                continue

            noise = rng.normal(0.0, 0.12, end - start)
            new_block = block * np.exp(noise)

            cand = working.copy()
            cand[start:end] = new_block
            cand = np.maximum(cand, 0.0)
            s = float(cand.sum())
            if s <= 1e-15:
                continue
            cand = cand / s

            c2 = conv_normalized(cand, N)
            sc = float(2.0 * n * c2.max())
            threshold = min(best_score, cur_score)
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < threshold - 1e-14:
                working = cand
                cur_score = sc
                improved = True
                if sc < best_score:
                    best_score = sc
                    best_a = cand.copy()

        if not improved:
            i = int(rng.integers(n))
            j = int(rng.integers(n))
            if i != j and working[i] > 1e-15:
                amt = 0.012 * working[i]
                cand = working.copy()
                cand[i] -= amt
                cand[j] += amt
                cand = np.maximum(cand, 0.0) / max(1e-300, cand.sum())
                c2 = conv_normalized(cand, N)
                sc = float(2.0 * n * c2.max())
                threshold = min(best_score, cur_score)
                if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < threshold - 1e-14:
                    working = cand
                    cur_score = sc
                    improved = True
                    if sc < best_score:
                        best_score = sc
                        best_a = cand.copy()

            stall += 1
            if stall == 60:
                working = best_a.copy()
            if stall > 140:
                break

    return best_a


def propose_candidate(budget_s=1800.0):
    """
    Main search entry point. Returns a list of non-negative floats.
    """
    t_global = time.monotonic()
    try:
        hard_limit = max(60.0, min(float(budget_s) - 90.0, 1710.0))
    except Exception:
        hard_limit = 1620.0

    best_seq = None
    best_score = float("inf")

    # Optional parent sequence from previous state of the art.
    parent_seq = None
    try:
        p = np.asarray(height_sequence_1, dtype=np.float64)
        if p.ndim != 1:
            p = p.reshape(-1)
        p = np.where(np.isfinite(p), p, 0.0)
        p = np.maximum(p, 0.0)
        sp = float(p.sum())
        if p.size > 0 and sp > 1e-10:
            parent_seq = p / sp
            q, sc = score_normalized(parent_seq)
            if q is not None and np.isfinite(sc):
                best_seq = q
                best_score = sc
    except Exception:
        parent_seq = None

    # Build a few cheap initial candidates around the central-binomial shape.
    candidate_lengths = [8192]
    if parent_seq is not None:
        lp = len(parent_seq)
        if 3000 <= lp <= 12000 and lp != 8192:
            candidate_lengths.append(lp)

    init_weights = [
        (0.0, 0.0, 0.0),
        (0.15, 0.0, 0.0),
        (-0.15, 0.0, 0.0),
        (0.0, 0.15, 0.0),
        (0.0, -0.15, 0.0),
        (0.25, 0.25, 0.0),
        (-0.25, 0.10, 0.0),
        (0.10, 0.0, 0.25),
    ]

    for n in candidate_lengths:
        if time.monotonic() - t_global > hard_limit - 10.0:
            break
        for b1, b2, b3 in init_weights:
            if time.monotonic() - t_global > hard_limit - 5.0:
                break
            try:
                cand = make_alpha_weighted(n, b1, b2, b3)
                best_seq, best_score = update_best(best_seq, best_score, cand)
            except Exception:
                pass

    # Fallback if everything failed.
    if best_seq is None:
        best_seq, best_score = score_normalized(make_alpha(4096))
        if best_seq is None:
            best_seq, best_score = score_normalized(np.ones(1024))

    # Low-dimensional shape refinement; then keep it in memory/up sample later.
    rem = hard_limit - (time.monotonic() - t_global)
    if rem > 160:
        low = lowdim_search(best_seq, min(140.0, rem * 0.16))
        rem = hard_limit - (time.monotonic() - t_global)
        if low is not None:
            work_n = len(best_seq)
            if not (3500 <= work_n <= 12000):
                work_n = 8192
            low_up = interpolate_sequence(low, work_n)
            best_seq, best_score = update_best(best_seq, best_score, low_up)

    # Choose a second high-dimensional candidate for diversity.
    cand2 = None
    if best_seq is not None:
        nbest = len(best_seq)
        if nbest < 8000:
            cand2 = interpolate_sequence(best_seq, 8192)
        elif nbest > 12000:
            cand2 = interpolate_sequence(best_seq, 8192)
        elif parent_seq is not None and len(parent_seq) != nbest:
            cand2 = parent_seq
        else:
            cand2 = make_alpha_weighted(8192, 0.07, 0.12, 0.0)

    # Main Gauss-Newton flattening on the best current candidate.
    rem = hard_limit - (time.monotonic() - t_global)
    if rem > 120:
        t1 = min(rem * 0.55, 820.0)
        if t1 > 40:
            opt = newton_optimize(best_seq, t1, verbose=True)
            best_seq, best_score = update_best(best_seq, best_score, opt)

    # After improving at one length, try a larger/alternate length derived from best.
    rem = hard_limit - (time.monotonic() - t_global)
    if rem > 90:
        if best_seq is not None and len(best_seq) < 8000:
            cand2 = interpolate_sequence(best_seq, 8192)
        elif cand2 is None:
            cand2 = make_alpha_weighted(8192, 0.05, 0.10, 0.0)

        if cand2 is not None:
            t2 = min(rem * 0.18, 320.0)
            if t2 > 30:
                opt = newton_optimize(cand2, t2, verbose=False)
                best_seq, best_score = update_best(best_seq, best_score, opt)

    # Squeeze if we are still above a safe margin below the target.
    rem = hard_limit - (time.monotonic() - t_global)
    while rem > 70 and np.isfinite(best_score) and best_score > 1.499:
        rem = hard_limit - (time.monotonic() - t_global)
        if rem < 70:
            break

        t3 = min(rem * 0.26, 220.0)
        if t3 > 25:
            opt = newton_optimize(best_seq, t3, verbose=False)
            best_seq, best_score = update_best(best_seq, best_score, opt)

        rem = hard_limit - (time.monotonic() - t_global)
        if rem < 45 or (np.isfinite(best_score) and best_score <= 1.499):
            break

        t4 = min(rem * 0.22, 150.0)
        if t4 > 20:
            top_k = 55 if len(best_seq) >= 4096 else 35
            opt = mgd_top(best_seq, t4, top_k=top_k, eta0=0.022)
            best_seq, best_score = update_best(best_seq, best_score, opt)

    # General local polishing with different mechanisms.
    rem = hard_limit - (time.monotonic() - t_global)
    if rem > 60:
        t5 = min(rem * 0.30, 260.0)
        if t5 > 30:
            top_k = 55 if len(best_seq) >= 4096 else 35
            opt = mgd_top(best_seq, t5, top_k=top_k, eta0=0.025)
            best_seq, best_score = update_best(best_seq, best_score, opt)

    rem = hard_limit - (time.monotonic() - t_global)
    if rem > 45:
        t6 = min(rem * 0.22, 130.0)
        if t6 > 20:
            bs = 40 if len(best_seq) >= 6000 else 24
            opt = block_search(best_seq, t6, block_size=bs)
            best_seq, best_score = update_best(best_seq, best_score, opt)

    # Final short Newton pass.
    rem = hard_limit - (time.monotonic() - t_global)
    if rem > 30:
        t7 = min(rem * 0.35, 110.0)
        if t7 > 15:
            opt = newton_optimize(best_seq, t7, verbose=False)
            best_seq, best_score = update_best(best_seq, best_score, opt)

    if best_seq is None:
        best_seq, best_score = score_normalized(make_alpha(4096))

    # Keep output length in a safe zone for the evaluator's direct convolution.
    if best_seq is not None:
        if len(best_seq) > 15000:
            best_seq = interpolate_sequence(best_seq, 12000)
        best_seq = np.asarray(best_seq, dtype=np.float64)
        best_seq = np.where(np.isfinite(best_seq), best_seq, 0.0)
        best_seq = np.maximum(best_seq, 0.0)
        s = float(best_seq.sum())
        if s <= 1e-12:
            best_seq, best_score = score_normalized(make_alpha(4096))
        else:
            best_seq = best_seq / s

    # Optional direct validation, avoiding the final seconds.
    try:
        elapsed = time.monotonic() - t_global
        if elapsed < hard_limit - 25:
            lst = [float(x) for x in best_seq]
            direct = evaluate_sequence(lst)
            if not np.isfinite(direct):
                # Extremely defensive fallback.
                bq, bsc = score_normalized(make_alpha(4096))
                if bq is not None and np.isfinite(bsc):
                    best_seq = bq
                    best_score = bsc
            else:
                # If direct is better/faster, keep the same vector.
                best_score = min(best_score, direct)
                print("Direct validation score: %.6f" % best_score)
    except Exception:
        pass

    print(
        "FINAL internal score: %.6f n=%s time=%.0fs"
        % (best_score, len(best_seq) if best_seq is not None else 0, time.monotonic() - t_global)
    )

    if best_seq is None:
        best_seq = make_alpha(1024)

    return [float(x) for x in best_seq]
```
